## assistant · 2026-07-23T13:59:49Z

我逐条对照了报告引用的实际代码（不仅仅是读报告本身），结论：**这份检视报告质量很高、结论基本可信，建议采纳**。核心 finding 我都在代码里找到了确凿证据，不是凭空推断：

## 已核实为真实 bug 的 4 个 High

1. **capability_tags 编辑不落库**（`adapter-config.ts:270` + `agent-config.ts` repository DTO）—— 确认属实：`AdapterConfigUpdateInput`（repository 层类型）根本没有 `capability_tags` 字段，service 的 `update()` 只用它算了 `updates.role`，从未写 `updates.capability_tags`。用户在 UI 勾选/取消能力标签，实际路由能力完全不变，是个能直接观察到的功能性 bug。

2. **validator Run 缺 `context_source_run_id`**（`workflow-service.ts:196`）—— 确认属实：`claimValidatorSlot()` 里创建 validator Run 的 `this.runRepo.create({...})` 调用确实没传这个字段；对照 `run-context-builder.ts:53-55` 的 `resolveContextSourceRunId()`，validator 角色一旦这个字段是 null 就直接返回 null，进而 `sectionHandoff()` 会拼出"这是第一个 Run，没有 prior handoff"这种自相矛盾的文案（因为 `assembleValidatorContext` 另外塞了正确的验证材料）。

3. **adapter availability 无法在运行期鉴权失败后收敛**（`run-dispatch.ts` / `agent-runner.ts:269`）—— 确认属实：`FailureReason` 枚举里根本没有 `AuthFailed` 这个值，全仓库只有 `adapter-config.ts` 两处调用 `agentConfigRepo.update`（都在 create/update/validate 路径里），`agent-runner.ts` 的终态处理完全没有任何地方把 adapter 标记为 unavailable。过期 OAuth/API key 会一直显示可用。

4. **OpenCode OAuth 的 validate() 与实际 dispatch 环境不一致**（`opencode-protocol.ts:61` vs `workspace-context.ts:97-112`）—— 确认属实：`validateOpenCodeCommand` 用的是 `env: { ...process.env, ...authEnv }`（完整真实环境），而 `workspace-context.ts` 里真实 dispatch 对 OpenCode + OAuth 的分支明确写了"no auth directory is exposed"、什么都不做。二者环境边界确实不同，会导致"探测可用"但"实际必败"。

## Medium/Low 也逐一核实属实

- 手动 validator 丢弃指令：`manual-routing-service.ts:88` 的 `dispatchValidator(issue.id, adapter.id)` 签名确实不接收 instructions，`trimmedInstructions`（line 72）被算出来后就没再用。
- Windows `shell=true`：`adapter-config.ts:47-51` 的 `validateCommand()` 确认还在用 `shell: process.platform === "win32"`。
- typecheck 不可复现：根 `package.json` 确认只有 `pretest`，没有 `pretypecheck`。
- `ThreadView.tsx:76` 的 `canSend` 确认只检查 terminal 状态和 instructions 非空，不检查 adapter 可用性。

## 建议

7 条 finding（含建议修复代码）没有发现失真或夸大，报告的 Bottom Line——"先修复 4 个 High 再进 done"——是合理的，可以直接按报告的 Suggested Fix 去改。如果要我着手修，建议顺序：① capability_tags 持久化 → ② context_source_run_id 绑定 → ③④ availability 收敛/OpenCode 环境一致性（这两个关联，可以一起改）→ 剩余 Medium/Low。需要我开始实施吗？

## assistant · 2026-07-23T15:15:35Z

recheck 报告的 5 条 finding 全部核实属实、已逐一修复:

**🟠 High(finding #1,AC-001 矛盾勾选)**:确认是我上一轮的疏漏——之前加的 Windows OpenCode OAuth guard 一律 fail closed,但实际上 `buildChildEnv()` 在 `push_credentials_enabled=true` 时会透传完整 `process.env`(不做隔离),这种场景下 OAuth 其实能跑通。找你确认方向后,按"workspace-aware"方案实施:
- `AdapterConfigService.validate(id, workspaceId?)` 新增可选 `workspaceId`,按目标 workspace 真实的 `push_credentials_enabled` 决定探测环境;跨 Project 或未提供时保守回退到隔离态。
- `RunDispatchService.reprobeAdapterOnFailure` 同样用失败 Run 自身的 workspace 真实配置去复核,不再一律假设隔离。
- UI 的 Validate 按钮暂未跟进(不选 workspace),这点已在文档里如实标注为后续工作,AC-001 保持未勾选。

**🟡 Medium 三条**:
- re-probe 之前排除了 Consult 角色——已改为不区分 role,只按 failure_reason 判断(availability 是 provider 属性,不该受 workflow role 影响)。
- fire-and-forget 复核有 TOCTOU 竞态(旧 probe 结果可能覆盖用户刚改好的新配置)——加了基于 `updated_at` 快照比对的 CAS 检查,过期结果直接丢弃。
- provider errorMessage 落库/回传前未脱敏——两个写入点(`AdapterConfigService.validate()` 和新的 re-probe)都接入了已有的 `redactSummary()`。

**🟢 Low(finding #5,文档过期)**:design.md §5.4 和 tasks.md T009 仍写着"OpenCode 用 XDG 变量可隔离"这个已被 Phase 13 推翻的结论,标记为 superseded 并写清真实结论。

全部验证:typecheck 全绿、web 155/155、生产构建成功;server 全量套件(新增 16 个回归测试用例)还在后台跑最终确认,完成后我会汇报结果。

我逐条对照代码核实了这份报告(以及中间那份我没见过的 `code-review-report-recheck-2.md`),结论:**质量很高,列出的问题基本都是真实的,建议采纳**,但有两点需要先跟你确认再动手,不能直接照单全收开始改。

## 核实结果

**已确认为真实 bug,可以直接修(无需决策)**:

| # | 严重度 | 问题 | 核实方式 |
|---|---|---|---|
| validator context 孤儿 Run | 🟠High | `claimValidatorSlot()` 里 context 构建失败时 catch 分支只是 `blockIssueInTx` 后 `return`,不抛异常——但整个流程包在 `db.transaction(() => {...})()` 里,回调正常返回=事务照常提交,已插入的 queued validator Run(空 instructions、无对应事件)会留在库里占住 per-round 唯一索引,后续无法重试 | 通读了 `blockIssueInTx` 实现,确认它不回滚不抛异常 |
| 跨 provider API key 泄漏 | 🟠High(安全) | `buildChildEnv()` 隔离模式的 denylist 只过滤 Git/SSH/token 相关变量,没有过滤 `OPENAI_API_KEY`/`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` 等 10 个 `OPENCODE_MODEL_PROVIDER_ENV` 里的 key——操作员环境里如果设了这些变量,任何 provider 的隔离 Run 都能读到 | 对照 `provider-metadata.ts` 的 allowlist 逐个确认未被过滤 |
| 空 `capability_tags` create 语义 | 🟡Medium | `create()` 用 `length > 0` 判断,显式传 `[]` 和不传效果一样都会被塞成 `[implementation]`;`update()` 用 `!== undefined` 判断,行为不一致。design.md 明确写了"空 capability 只能 consult"这条规则 | 对照 design.md 第 476 行规则确认 |
| `purpose` 等字段无运行时校验 | 🟡Medium | `RUN_PURPOSE_INVALID` 错误码存在、有单测断言映射到 400、design.md 里也写了用途,但 `runs.ts` 路由从没抛过这个错——非法值直接静默当 `undefined`(auto)处理 | 三处交叉确认(错误码定义、错误码测试、design 文档),路由代码确实没用上 |
| 编辑页无法清空字段 | 🟡Medium | Web 端清空后发 `undefined`,后端把 `undefined` 解释为"保留原值";create/update 语义不对称 | 对照两端代码确认 |
| 删除 204 响应 | 🟡Medium | `apiFetch()` 对所有 `res.ok` 无条件 `res.json()`,204 空 body 会抛 SyntaxError,导致"删除其实成功了但 UI 报错" | 确认路由返回 204、client 无 204 分支 |

| sanitizer 遗漏多数 provider key 格式 | 🟡Medium | 正则只认 `sk-`/`gh*`/`xox*`/`AKIA`,OpenCode 支持的 Google/DeepSeek/OpenRouter 等 key 格式不在列 | 对照 allowlist 确认 |
| validator 提示忽略可用性 | 🟢Low | 只要有一个 validator capability 的 adapter 就不提示,不管它是不是 Unavailable | 确认逻辑没查 status |

## 需要先决策的两点

**1. workspace-aware probe 写全局状态(🟠High,报告里的第 2 条)** ——这条是在说我上一轮加的 workspace-aware 修复本身不完整:`AdapterConfigService.validate(id, workspaceId)` 探测时确实按目标 workspace 的 `push_credentials_enabled` 判断,但结果写回的是 **Project 级全局** `agent_configs.status`。如果对一个 `push_credentials_enabled=true` 的 workspace 探测成功,adapter 会被标为全局 Available,同一 Project 下其它隔离 workspace 的 resolver 也会认为它可用——但实际会失败。

我分析下来,这个风险目前**理论存在但还打不到**:Web 的 Validate 按钮还没传 `workspace_id`(我上次就写明是已知缺口),所以现在没有 UI 路径能触发这个"错误放行"。而失败后自动复核那条路径(`reprobeAdapterOnFailure`)其实是安全的——它只会降级,不会因为某个 workspace 探测成功就把全局状态提升为 Available。真正有风险的只有显式 `validate(id, workspaceId)` 这一个入口。

**2. shutdown 时收敛任务丢失(🟡Medium)** ——这条要接入 server 生命周期/shutdown hook,我还没看过现有 server 有没有这类基础设施,需要先看代码再评估工作量。

现在把所有遗留问题整理成一份清单,分三类。

## 1. 产品/架构层面(有意保留,不是遗漏)

| 项 | 现状 | 为什么没动 |
|---|---|---|
| **AC-001 · 初始可用性语义** | `create()`/`update()` 时 `AdapterStatus.Available` 仍只代表"命令能被 `resolveExecutable()` 解析",不代表已鉴权。未登录的 Claude/OpenCode adapter 仍可能被自动设为 Project default。 | 改成 `Unknown` 默认会牵连自动设默认 adapter 逻辑和大量既有测试的行为语义,你在最早讨论时就选择先不做这个更大的改动。 |
| **AC-001 · workspace 级 availability 模型** | Availability 仍是单一 Project 级全局状态。`validate(id, workspaceId)`/失败 re-probe 已经避免"某个 workspace 许可就把全局标成 Available"这个错误方向,但反过来"该 workspace 自己验证成功后应该能路由""某 workspace 失败不该连累其它 workspace"这两个方向仍未解决,需要把状态存储维度从 adapter 扩展到 `(adapter, workspace)`。 | 七轮报告里最后两轮都重复确认这一条,报告本身建议的方案是新增一张表 + 改 resolver/ValidatorSelector 签名——这是功能级新增,不是"修 bug",需要你决定要不要做、什么时候做。 |
| **Adapter Settings UI 不支持按 workspace 选择探测** | Validate 按钮不传 `workspace_id`,后端已经支持的 workspace-aware 探测能力目前从 UI 侧不可达。 | 同上,UI 概念(同一 adapter 在不同 workspace 下可能显示不同可用状态)还没设计,是这条架构决策的下游。 |

## 2. 已知但认为影响可接受的小缺口

- **真实浏览器人工点击验证未做**——本环境没有浏览器工具,Composer/Thread/Inspector 等 UI 全程只有自动化测试覆盖,没有人工过一遍。
- **凭据隔离 allowlist 本质仍是一份人工维护的名单**(哪怕已经从 denylist 换成 allowlist、也修了 proxy URL 凭据泄漏)——如果某个 CLI 未来需要一个新的基础设施变量,需要手动加进 `SAFE_PARENT_ENV_NAMES`,不是自动发现的。

## 3. 流程性收尾(随时可以处理,不是代码问题)

- **7 份检视报告文件未 commit**:`code-review-report-implementation.md`、`recheck.md`、`recheck-2.md`、`final-comprehensive.md`、`final-recheck.md`、`final-recheck-2.md`、`final-recheck-3.md`
- **这一整批代码/测试/文档改动(61 个文件)都还没 commit**

---

除此之外,typecheck、server 1336 项、web 162 项测试、生产构建全部保持绿色,没有已知的功能性 bug 残留。要不要现在处理第 3 类(commit 或整理这些报告文件)?

## 2. Key Technical Concepts

- **PersonaHub monorepo**: npm workspaces (`shared`/`server`/`web`), Fastify + better-sqlite3 backend, Vite+React frontend, TypeScript throughout.
- **F005 feature**: Manual multi-agent routing across Codex/Claude Code/OpenCode CLI adapters, with credential isolation, validator workflows, and adapter availability tracking.
- **Credential isolation** (`server/src/runtime/workspace-context.ts`, `buildChildEnv()`): builds a child process env for spawned CLI adapters; evolved from denylist → allowlist (`SAFE_PARENT_ENV_NAMES`) architecture; proxy URL userinfo credential stripping (`isSafeProxyValue()`).
- **AdapterStatus enum**: `Unknown | Available | Unavailable` — `Unknown` means "resolvable but not confirmed via real auth probe."
- **Zod** for HTTP route runtime validation (already a dependency, per `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`'s mandate that routes do `参数校验（zod）`).
- **Async convergence pattern**: fire-and-forget but *tracked* background probes (`pendingAvailabilityProbes: Set<Promise<void>>`, `trackAvailabilityProbe()`, `shutdown(timeoutMs)`) used in both `RunDispatchService` (existing) and now `AdapterConfigService` (new) — logs failures via `console.warn` instead of silently swallowing, and lets server shutdown drain pending probes with a bounded timeout.
- **Workspace-aware "effective status" model** (new, in progress): `agent_configs.status` = Project-global conservative baseline; new `adapter_workspace_status` table = per-`(adapter,workspace)` **exception override**; `effectiveAdapterStatus(record, override)` = `override?.status ?? record.status`.
- **Real-CLI env-gated tests**: `REAL_CODEX`, `REAL_CLAUDE`, `REAL_OPENCODE` env vars gate `describe.skipIf(!REAL)` blocks in `server/tests/integration/real-*.test.ts` files, used for genuine validation against this machine's real logged-in CLIs.
- **Fake CLI fixture scripts**: `server/tests/helpers/fake-{codex,claude,opencode}.mjs`, controlled via `FAKE_{CODEX,CLAUDE,OPENCODE}_MODE` env vars (or, after the allowlist fix broke env-var passthrough under isolation, via `process.argv[2]` matched against a `KNOWN_MODES` allowlist as a fallback).
- **Migration convention**: `server/src/db/schema-v{N}.ts` files + `server/src/db/migrations.ts`'s `applyMigrations()` incrementing `schema_version` table.
- **better-sqlite3 with `PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON`**: FK constraints are enforced; no `ON DELETE CASCADE` used anywhere in existing schema (matches convention of explicit app-layer cleanup, e.g. `hasRuns()` checks before delete).

```ts
export const SCHEMA_V7 = `
-- Workspace-aware adapter availability (F005 code-review follow-up,
-- 2026-07-24). agent_configs.status stays the Project-global, conservative
-- baseline (used whenever no workspace is named) — this table only holds
-- EXCEPTIONS for a specific (adapter_config_id, workspace_id) pair whose
-- effective availability genuinely differs from that baseline (e.g. an
-- OpenCode OAuth adapter that's globally Unknown but confirmed Available in
-- one specific workspace with push_credentials_enabled=true). No row here
-- for a given pair means "no override — use the global status".

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS adapter_workspace_status (
  adapter_config_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agent_configs(id),
  workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES workspaces(id),
  status TEXT NOT NULL,
  last_checked_at TEXT,
  auth_status_message TEXT,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (adapter_config_id, workspace_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_adapter_workspace_status_workspace
  ON adapter_workspace_status(workspace_id);
`;
```

```ts
import { AdapterStatus, type AgentCapability } from "@personahub/shared/types";
import type { AgentConfigRecord } from "../repositories/agent-config.js";
import { hasCapability } from "../repositories/agent-config.js";
import type { AdapterWorkspaceStatusRecord } from "../repositories/adapter-workspace-status.js";

export function effectiveAdapterStatus(
  record: { status: AdapterStatus },
  override: AdapterWorkspaceStatusRecord | null,
): AdapterStatus {
  return override?.status ?? record.status;
}

export function listAvailableByCapabilityForWorkspace(
  candidates: AgentConfigRecord[],
  overrides: AdapterWorkspaceStatusRecord[],
  capability: AgentCapability,
): AgentConfigRecord[] {
  const overrideByAdapterId = new Map(overrides.map((o) => [o.adapter_config_id, o]));
  return candidates
    .filter((c) => hasCapability(c, capability))
    .filter((c) => effectiveAdapterStatus(c, overrideByAdapterId.get(c.id) ?? null) === AdapterStatus.Available)
    .sort((a, b) => (a.created_at < b.created_at ? -1 : a.created_at > b.created_at ? 1 : a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1));
}
```

```ts
export class AdapterConfigService {
  private pendingAvailabilityProbes = new Set<Promise<void>>();

  constructor(
    private agentConfigRepo: AgentConfigRepository,
    private projectRepo: ProjectRepository,
    private adapterRegistry: AgentAdapterRegistry,
    private workspaceRepo: WorkspaceRepository,
    private adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo: AdapterWorkspaceStatusRepository,
  ) {}

  private trackAvailabilityProbe(probe: Promise<void>): void {
    this.pendingAvailabilityProbes.add(probe);
    void probe
      .catch((error) => { console.warn("[AdapterConfigService] auto-validate after create/update failed:", error); })
      .finally(() => { this.pendingAvailabilityProbes.delete(probe); });
  }

  async shutdown(timeoutMs = 5_000): Promise<void> {
    if (this.pendingAvailabilityProbes.size === 0) return;
    const pending = Promise.allSettled([...this.pendingAvailabilityProbes]);
    const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));
    await Promise.race([pending, timeout]);
  }

  private async autoValidateAfterCreate(adapterId: string, projectId: string, tryMakeDefault: boolean): Promise<void> {
    let validated: AdapterConfig;
    try { validated = await this.validate(adapterId); } catch { return; }
    if (validated.status !== AS.Available) return;
    const project = this.projectRepo.getById(projectId);
    if (!project) return;
    if (tryMakeDefault || project.default_adapter_config_id === null) {
      this.projectRepo.setDefaultAdapter(projectId, adapterId);
    }
  }

  private async autoValidateAfterUpdate(adapterId: string): Promise<void> {
    try { await this.validate(adapterId); } catch { /* nothing to converge */ }
  }
  // ... create()/update()/validate() etc. follow
```

- **`validate()` method — THIS IS WHERE I WAS INTERRUPTED.** The method currently (as last read, NOT YET MODIFIED for the override-table write) still contains the OLD logic from the previous round:
```ts
async validate(id: string, workspaceId?: string): Promise<AdapterConfig> {
  const existing = this.agentConfigRepo.getById(id);
  if (!existing) { throw new AppError(ErrorCode.ADAPTER_NOT_FOUND, "Adapter config not found."); }
  const project = this.projectRepo.getById(existing.project_id);
  const publicConfig = toPublicAdapter(existing, project?.default_adapter_config_id ?? null);
  const adapter = this.adapterRegistry.getForConfig(publicConfig);
  const workspace = workspaceId ? this.workspaceRepo.getById(workspaceId) : null;
  const pushCredentialsEnabled = workspace?.project_id === existing.project_id
    ? workspace.push_credentials_enabled
    : false;
  const result = await adapter.validate(publicConfig, existing.api_key, { pushCredentialsEnabled });

  // Availability is persisted as a single Project-scoped status, shared by
  // every workspace in the Project — a probe that only succeeded because
  // THIS workspace happens to have push_credentials_enabled=true must not
  // promote that to a blanket "Available" the resolver/selector would then
  // trust for other (isolated) workspaces too, where it would actually
  // fail. `Unknown` says exactly what's true: confirmed working under this
  // one workspace's conditions, not confirmed globally. A conservative
  // (isolated, or no workspace named) probe result is unaffected — that
  // one *is* globally representative.
  const status: AdapterStatus = result.available
    ? (pushCredentialsEnabled ? AS.Unknown : AS.Available)
    : AS.Unavailable;
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  this.agentConfigRepo.update(id, {
    status,
    last_checked_at: now,
    auth_status_message: sanitizeAuthStatusMessage(result.errorMessage, [existing.api_key]),
    updated_at: now,
  });

  const record = this.agentConfigRepo.getById(id)!;
  return toPublicAdapter(record, project?.default_adapter_config_id ?? null);
}
```
This STILL needs to be rewritten per the new "exception override" design agreed with the user: when `workspaceId` is provided and belongs to the project, the result should be written to `this.adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo.upsert(...)` (NOT to `agentConfigRepo.update()`'s global status), and the returned DTO should reflect the workspace-specific effective status (constructed manually, not re-read from the global record). When `workspaceId` is omitted (or doesn't belong to the project), behavior should stay exactly as today (write to global `agentConfigRepo.update()`).

- **Stale/mid-edit TypeScript diagnostics**: Throughout the session, intermediate diagnostic messages like "'X' is declared but its value is never read" appeared after single edits in a multi-edit sequence; these consistently resolved themselves once the corresponding usage edit was also applied. I learned to treat these as expected noise during multi-step edits, not real errors, and verified with full `tsc --noEmit` runs at natural checkpoints.
- **`adapter-resolver.ts` string-literal vs enum mismatch**: When I first edited `adapter-resolver.ts` I mistakenly left a `!== "available"` string literal instead of `!== AdapterStatus.Available` after removing/re-adding the `AdapterStatus` import; fixed via `sed -i 's/!== "available"/!== AdapterStatus.Available/g'` and re-adding the `AdapterStatus` import. A system-reminder noted this file was modified externally (by a linter/formatter) — I was instructed not to revert this and to take it into account, which I did (the sed fix was compatible with and built upon that external change).
- **`updates` type object missing `auth_status_message` field** in `adapter-config.ts`'s `update()` method: after adding `updates.auth_status_message = null;` inside the command-change block, TypeScript flagged `Property 'auth_status_message' does not exist on type '{...}'` because the local type annotation for `updates` didn't declare that field. Fixed by adding `auth_status_message?: string | null;` to the type annotation.
- **(Earlier round, for context)** The credential-isolation allowlist conversion broke 22 tests across 4 files because `FAKE_{CODEX,CLAUDE,OPENCODE}_MODE` env vars no longer reached fake CLI child processes under isolation. Root-caused precisely (fake scripts read `process.env.FAKE_CODEX_MODE`, but the new allowlist strips unlisted env vars). Fixed initially by setting `push_credentials_enabled=true` in the shared `setupIssue()` test helpers — but this broke 2 OTHER tests specifically testing `CredentialIsolationBlocked` escalation (which require isolation to be ACTIVE to correctly trigger). Fixed those 2 tests surgically by passing the mode via `args: ["credential_failure"]` (real argv, unaffected by env filtering) instead, and updating `fake-claude.mjs`/`fake-opencode.mjs` to check `process.argv[2]` against a `KNOWN_MODES` allowlist set (not a generic "doesn't start with -" heuristic, which itself caused a SECOND round of 10 new failures because OpenCode's real argv always has `"run"` as a non-flag positional token at that position — fixed by using an explicit `KNOWN_MODES` Set instead of the heuristic).
- **My own earlier "tail-truncated" background test output**: Running `npm -w @personahub/server run test 2>&1 | tail -20` in a background task silently discarded the detailed per-test failure output, making the initial regression (22 failures) hard to diagnose from the log alone; I had to re-run the specific suspected files directly (without `tail`) to see full stack traces. Learned to avoid piping background test runs through `tail` when failures are possible.

## 5. Problem Solving

- Established a rigorous "verify before trust" methodology for code-review reports across 7 rounds: every finding was checked against actual source via Read/Grep before being accepted or rejected, catching that some findings were accurate, some were already-known deferred items being re-flagged (not new), and none were outright fabricated.
- Correctly distinguished between "quick, safe, narrowly-scoped bug fixes" (implemented immediately without asking) versus "genuine product/architecture trade-offs" (paused and used `AskUserQuestion` before proceeding) — this distinction was applied consistently and is the core reason the two AC-001/workspace-model items were left deliberately unaddressed for many rounds until the user explicitly asked to implement the suggested solutions.
- Investigated actual blast radius before committing to an implementation approach for the two deferred items: discovered only 6 test files call `adapterConfigService.create()` (vs. 61 bypassing via direct repository access), and only 1 call site exists for `resolveAdapter()` — this made the previously-feared "huge blast radius" concern much more tractable and informed the decision to proceed with implementation now.
- Designed the "exception override" workspace-status model specifically to be additive/non-breaking: `agent_configs.status` remains authoritative for all adapters that never need workspace-dependent behavior (the vast majority — Codex, Claude), while the new table only ever gets rows for genuinely workspace-dependent cases (OpenCode+Windows+OAuth), preserving all existing test assumptions about global status.
- Real-CLI validation discipline maintained: whenever a change touched credential isolation or dispatch env-building, I ran real, env-gated integration tests against actual logged-in Codex/Claude/OpenCode CLIs on this machine to confirm no functional regression, not just unit-test-level mocked verification.

## 8. Current Work

Immediately before this summary was requested, I was in the middle of implementing the workspace-aware availability model (Part 2) together with the AC-001 async-validate fix (Part 1), per the user's explicit direction: "两个遗留问题在检视文档里给出了解决方案，看看如何" followed by two `AskUserQuestion` clarifications where the user selected:
- AC-001: "折中：create/update 后异步自动 validate(推荐)" (middle-ground: async auto-validate after create/update — recommended)
- Workspace model: "新表作为'例外覆盖'，回退到全局 status(推荐)" (new table as "exception override", falls back to global status — recommended)

I had completed:
1. Created `server/src/db/schema-v7.ts` with the `adapter_workspace_status` table DDL.
2. Wired it into `server/src/db/migrations.ts`.
3. Created `server/src/repositories/adapter-workspace-status.ts` with `AdapterWorkspaceStatusRepository` (get/listForWorkspace/upsert/deleteForAdapter methods).
4. Created `server/src/services/adapter-availability.ts` with the pure helpers `effectiveAdapterStatus()` and `listAvailableByCapabilityForWorkspace()`.
5. Updated `server/src/services/adapter-resolver.ts` — `resolveAdapter()` now takes a required `workspaceId` param and uses `effectiveAdapterStatus()`.
6. Updated `server/src/services/manual-routing-service.ts` — constructor takes `adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo`, call site passes `issue.workspace_id` to `resolveAdapter()`.
7. Updated `server/src/services/validation/workflow-service.ts` — constructor takes `adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo` (inserted before the defaulted `manualValidatorGraceMs` param), both auto-mode (via `listAvailableByCapabilityForWorkspace()`) and explicit-mode (via `effectiveAdapterStatus()`) validator selection now workspace-aware.
8. Updated `server/src/index.ts` — added `AdapterWorkspaceStatusRepository` import, instantiated `adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo`, passed it into `AdapterConfigService`, `ValidationWorkflowService`, and `ManualRoutingService` constructors.
9. Extensively modified `server/src/services/adapter-config.ts`:
   - Added `pendingAvailabilityProbes`, `trackAvailabilityProbe()`, `shutdown()` (mirroring `RunDispatchService`'s pattern).
   - Added `autoValidateAfterCreate()` and `autoValidateAfterUpdate()` private async methods.
   - Constructor now takes `adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo` as 5th param.
   - `create()`: status computation changed from `Available` to `Unknown` for a resolvable command; removed synchronous default-adapter assignment; added `trackAvailabilityProbe(autoValidateAfterCreate(...))` call when status is Unknown.
   - `update()`: `updates` type gained `auth_status_message` field; command-change block now sets `Unknown` instead of `Available` and clears `last_checked_at`/`auth_status_message`; added a new invalidation block for auth-related-field-only changes (recomputes status via `validateCommand(existing.command)` when `updates.status === undefined`); added `trackAvailabilityProbe(autoValidateAfterUpdate(id))` call at the end when `updates.status === AS.Unknown`.

I had just re-read the CURRENT (still old/unmodified) `validate()` method implementation via the Read tool — this was the very last action before the summary request interrupted the session. The read output showed the existing `validate()` method (lines ~510-563 in the file at that point) still containing the PREVIOUS round's logic that writes workspace-permissive results as `AS.Unknown` to the GLOBAL `agentConfigRepo.update()` call — this is exactly what needs to change to fulfill the new "exception override" design: workspace-scoped results should go to `adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo.upsert()` instead, leaving global status untouched, while the no-`workspaceId` case continues writing to global status exactly as before.

Numerous outstanding TypeScript compile errors exist at this point (multiple constructor call sites not yet updated with the new `adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo` argument — see Section 3 and 7 for the exact list), and no test runs or typecheck runs have been performed since this batch of changes began (the previous full-green baseline was 1336/1336 server tests, 162/162 web tests, before this round of work started).

## 9. Optional Next Step

The immediate next step, directly continuing the exact work interrupted, is to finish rewriting the `AdapterConfigService.validate()` method in `server/src/services/adapter-config.ts` to implement the "exception override" write-path design just agreed with the user, replacing this current logic:

```ts
const status: AdapterStatus = result.available
  ? (pushCredentialsEnabled ? AS.Unknown : AS.Available)
  : AS.Unavailable;
const now = new Date().toISOString();

this.agentConfigRepo.update(id, {
  status,
  last_checked_at: now,
  auth_status_message: sanitizeAuthStatusMessage(result.errorMessage, [existing.api_key]),
  updated_at: now,
});

const record = this.agentConfigRepo.getById(id)!;
return toPublicAdapter(record, project?.default_adapter_config_id ?? null);
```

with logic that, when `workspaceId` is given and belongs to the project, upserts into `this.adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo` instead of touching the global column, and constructs/returns a DTO reflecting that workspace-specific effective status — while the no-`workspaceId` (or cross-project) path continues writing to global `agentConfigRepo.update()` exactly as before (zero behavior change for that path, preserving all existing tests that call `validate(id)` without a workspace).

After that, the next steps in strict order are: (a) add `adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo.deleteForAdapter(id)` to `delete()`; (b) update `RunDispatchService.reprobeAdapterOnFailure()` to write overrides instead of global status; (c) fix all remaining constructor call sites (`tests/helpers.ts` and the 5 `ValidationWorkflowService`-constructing test files) to pass the new repo; (d) run `npm run typecheck` to find and fix all remaining compile errors; (e) run the full server/web test suites to find and fix behavioral regressions (especially the 6 files using `adapterConfigService.create()` directly and the HTTP route tests asserting immediate `is_default`/`Available`); (f) add new regression tests for both the AC-001 async-convergence behavior and the workspace-override behavior; (g) run real-CLI validation given the credential/dispatch-adjacent nature of the changes; (h) update documentation (spec.md/design.md/tasks.md/CLAUDE.md and the review report annotations) to reflect these two items as now resolved.

## assistant · 2026-07-24T16:39:23Z

I verified all 7 findings against the actual code — all are accurate, not fabricated. Summary of what I confirmed:

- **4 High**: (1) `update()` invalidates the global status on auth/command changes but never clears stale `adapter_workspace_status` override rows — a workspace that validated `Available` under an old command/key stays `Available` after the config changes underneath it; `args` changes don't even invalidate the global status. (2) `validate()` has no stale-result guard (unlike `reprobeAdapterOnFailure()`, which already snapshots `updated_at`) — a slow probe can silently overwrite a newer edit. (3) The workspace-aware model has no UI/API closure — the Validate button never sends `workspace_id`, the adapter list endpoint has no workspace param — so a real user can never actually observe the Windows+OpenCode+OAuth fix I just built. My own `spec.md` wording ("AC-001 已完全满足") is inconsistent with that gap. (4) An invalid or cross-Project `workspace_id` passed to `validate()` silently falls through to a **global** write instead of erroring.
- **2 Medium**: server shutdown never awaits `AdapterConfigService.shutdown()`; a delayed `make_default` probe can clobber a newer explicit default choice.
- **1 Low**: the "exception-only" table comment doesn't match actual behavior (always upserts, even when equal to baseline).

Findings 1, 2, 4, 5 (shutdown), 6 (make_default race), and 7 (doc/behavior mismatch) are clear correctness bugs — I'll fix these directly. Finding 3 has an honest-wording part I'll fix now regardless (walk back the "已完全满足" overclaim), but whether to actually build the UI/API workspace-selection surface now is new scope, not a bug fix.

## assistant · 2026-07-25T16:49:56Z

`code-review-report-final-closure-recheck.md` 中的 2 项 High（以及一并核实的 3 Medium + 1 Low）均已核实修复：

**2 项 High**
1. **`args` 单独变化仍未使全局 status 失效**——之前只清了 workspace override，全局 `Available` 保留、无需重新 probe 即可继续路由。`update()` 已重构为统一失效判定：先算出 `availabilityRelevantFieldsTouched`（现含 `args`），命中即统一走一次探测计算并写 `status`，不再按字段散落判断。
2. **global `validate()` 无并发写序保护**——两个探测针对同一未变配置重叠时，谁"写得晚"谁赢，与代码注释宣称的行为不符。新增 schema v7 `agent_configs.availability_revision`（单调 CAS token，`update()` 每次失效时递增），`validate()` 全局路径改为 `AgentConfigRepository.casUpdateAvailability(id, snapshotRevision, patch)`——条件 UPDATE，落败探测影响 0 行。这个机制同时替换了之前"按字段快照判断变化"的方案，天然规避了此前对 `capability_tags`-only 编辑的误判。

**顺带修复的 3 Medium + 1 Low**：`update()`/`delete()` 补上事务原子性；scoped `validate()` 补上 workspace 自身 `updated_at` 快照（`push_credentials_enabled` 中途变化也会使结果失效）；workspace-scoped list API 补齐 5 个 server 侧测试。"scoped probe 结果等于基线时丢失该 workspace 独立 last_checked_at" 一项判定为可接受的设计取舍（例外覆盖表语义下的自然结果），未改动，已在文档中记录理由。

**验证**：server 1378、web 164 测试全绿，typecheck、生产构建通过；新增的两个重叠探测竞态测试（global + workspace-scoped）重复运行 3 次无 flake；真实已登录 Codex/Claude/OpenCode CLI 再次追加验收（4 个 validate 相关真实 CLI 测试文件全过）。

两项原始遗留 finding（AC-001 语义、workspace-aware availability）现已真正闭环，`spec.md`/`CLAUDE.md` 已同步更新。仍未提交。

## assistant · 2026-07-25T17:30:40Z

`code-review-report-final-closure-recheck-2.md` 中的 2 项 High（以及一并核实的 1 Medium + 1 Low）均已核实修复：

**2 项 High（同一根因，一并解决）**
1. **新 CAS 列追加进已跑过的 schema v7**——已用真实本机 `personahub.db` 验证这类风险确实存在。
2. **CAS 只实现"先完成者获胜"，不是"后发起者获胜"**——上一轮的自测只覆盖了"后调用但先完成"这一种巧合顺序，没测反向情形。

复核后判断，DB 持久化 revision 本身就是不必要的方案——这类"覆盖同进程内在途探测竞争"的问题只在进程存活期间有意义，重启后没有任何东西"仍在进行中"，跟本模块已有的 `pendingAvailabilityProbes`、workspace 内存锁是同一类协调机制，不该落库。已撤销该列，改为 `AdapterConfigService` 内两张纯内存 Map：`configGenerations`（按 adapterId，配置失效时递增）+ `probeGenerations`（按 scope key，**调用开始时**领取而非完成时才检查）。这同时解决了两项 High：不再有可能漏迁移的列，且语义变成正确的"最近一次调用胜出"。

**顺带修复**：workspace snapshot 从整个 `updated_at` 收窄为只比较 `push_credentials_enabled`（Medium，避免被 lock/branch 等无关写误伤）；`design.md` 更新为当前事实（Low）。

**验证**：新增双向完成顺序测试（B 后调用先完成 / A 先调用先完成，均验证后调用者胜出，含 scoped 路径删除分支的情形）覆盖了旧方案会漏掉的场景，重复运行 3 次无 flake；server 1382、web 164 测试全绿，typecheck、生产构建通过；真实已登录 Codex/Claude/OpenCode CLI 再次追加验收。两项原始遗留 finding 现已真正闭环，`spec.md`/`CLAUDE.md`/前两轮报告均已同步更新交叉引用。仍未提交。

回到您实际的问题——如何打开 Claude Code 的文件删除（`rm`）权限：

**方式一：交互时直接放行**
下次弹出权限确认提示时，选择"允许"（如果界面提供"本次会话始终允许"之类的选项，选那个即可让后续同类操作不再询问）。

**方式二：在项目/用户级 settings 里配置允许规则**
Claude Code 的权限规则存放在 settings 文件里，按优先级通常有：
- 项目级：`.claude/settings.json`（会入库）或 `.claude/settings.local.json`（仅本机，你们仓库的 `.gitignore` 里已经排除了这个文件，说明本地已经在用它）
- 用户级：`~/.claude/settings.json`

在对应文件的 `permissions.allow` 数组里加一条类似规则即可放行 `rm`：

```json
{
  "permissions": {
    "allow": ["Bash(rm:*)"]
  }
}
```

如果想更保守一点，可以只放行特定路径，例如 `Bash(rm docs/features/0.1/F005-multi-agent-manual-routing/*)`，这样只对这个目录下的删除生效，其它路径的 `rm` 仍会询问。

**方式三：切换权限模式**
如果当前是逐条确认的模式，也可以切换到会自动放行的模式（如"Accept Edits"一类），但这个会放开的范围比只放行 `rm` 更大，风险也更高，个人机器上按需选择就好。

配置好之后，我再重新执行删除应该就不会被拦了。
